def eval_smooth(z, beta):
   m=z.max(); exp=np.exp(z-m); a=exp/exp.sum()
   b=conv_square(a) (FFT)
   mb=b.max()
   ex=np.exp(beta*(b-mb)); p=ex/ex.sum()
   f = mb + np.log(ex.sum()) / beta
   # gradient wrt a:
   w=p
   h=crosscorr(a, w) via FFT length 3n
   ga=2*h
   gz=ga - a*np.dot(a,ga)
   return f, gz
```

Let's design **custom L-BFGS with Ying?** Maybe easier 50 lines. Two-loop recursion:
```
def lbfgs_direction(g, m, s_hist, y_hist, rho_hist):
    q = g.copy()
    alpha = np.zeros(m)
    for k in reversed(range(m)):
        alpha[k] = rho_hist[k] * np.dot(s_hist[k], q)
        q -= alpha[k]*y_hist[k]
    r = q / np.dot(y_hist[-1], q)? Actually if m>0: if last satisfies curvature, scale by (s_last·q)/(y_last·q); else scale? Standard:
    if m>0:
       yk=s? Use last stored.
       denom = np.dot(yk,q)
       if denom <= 1e-12: r = q / np.linalg.norm(q) maybe; else: r = q * np.dot(sk,q)/denom
    else: r = -q? Actually descent: if m=0, r = q/gnorm? Standard H0=I, direction = -g.
    for k in range(m):
       beta = rho_hist[k]*np.dot(y_hist[k], r)
       r += (alpha[k]-beta)*s_hist[k]
    # if not descent, set r=-g
```
Need maintain pairs only if s·y>0 and f decrease? L-BFGS with nonconvex can have poor curvature; use damping or discard. Simpler: use m=10 and reset history if dot <=0. If no positive curvature, direction = -g.

Line search: Armijo with backtracking. Use `c1=1e-4`. `slope = g@p` (should <0). Try α=prev_alpha or 1. For t: x=z+t p; fnew=gz@? If fnew <= f + c1*t*slope, accept. Else t*=0.5. Max 40. If fail, reduce p (restart: z=best_z, history=[], f=best_f,g=best_g). Also maybe use adaptive initial α from previous accepted.

After accept, update history: s=t p; y=g_new-g; sy=y·s; if sy>1e-14: append, pop if >m. Else maybe discard. Update alpha history? Standard.

Objective S depends on β; if β changes during run, history invalid. Anneal β by resetting history when β changes. We can do continuous increase of β: every e.g. 30s or 100 iters increase β by factor 1.5, reset history. Or just run separate chunks with fixed β, carry best z. This is robust.

Stage for each start:
```

Convolution function:
```
def square_conv(a, nfft):
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, n=nfft)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * np.conj(fa), n=nfft)[:2*len(a)-1]
    # realir maybe
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    s = c.sum()
    if s > 0: c /= s
    return c
```
Use `fa * fa.conj()` real? it's complex; `np.fft.irfft`. Could use `fa * fa` since conj? Square magnitude = fa.real^2+fa.imag^2; `fa * np.conj(fa)` okay. `irfft` returns real.

Crosscorr:
```
def weighted_grad(a, w, nfft):
    L = len(w)
    n = len(a)
    w_rev = w[::-1]
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, n=nfft)
    fw = np.fft.rfft(w_rev, n=nfft)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(fa * fw, n=nfft)
    # Need indices L-1-i; since conv length nL = n+L-1
    idx = L - 1 - np.arange(n)
    h = conv[idx]
    # Because circular conv with Ng≥n+L-1, direct. idx nonnegative.
    return 2*h
```
Check length: conv has Ng elements; first n+L-1 = 3n-2 valid. idx max L-1=2n-2 <3n-2. Good.
Potential issue: `w_rev` length L, rfft pads zeros. Fine.

def z_to_a(z):
    zmax = np.max(z)
    ez = np.exp(z - zmax)
    s = ez.sum()
    if s ==0 or not finite: # fallback uniform
       ez = np.ones_like(z); s=len(z)
    return ez/s
```

def lbfgs_run(a0, budget, rng, beta_schedule, target_score):
    z = np.log(np.maximum(a0, 1e-300))
    z -= z.max()
    f, score, g = smooth_score_grad(z, beta...)
    best_f=f; best_score=score; best_z=z.copy(); best_a=a0.copy()? but evaluate best from z.
    history arrays as lists of (s,y,rho)
    alpha_prev=1.0
    stall_time = time
    failed=0
    total_deadline=...
    for beta in schedule:
       deadline_beta = min(total_deadline, start + beta_budget)
       reset history
       while time<deadline_beta:
          # compute direction
          p = two_loop(-g?) Actually two_loop expects g and returns d (descent). We'll pass g.
          # ensure direction descent: slope = dot(g,p); if slope>=0: p = -g; slope = -dot(g,g)
          if not finite: restart.
          # line search
          t=alpha_prev
          accepted=False
          for _ in range(60):
             z_new = z + t*p
             # optional center z_new
             z_new -= z_new.max()
             f_new, score_new, g_new = smooth_score_grad(z_new, beta,...)
             if f_new <= f + 1e-4*t*slope + 1e-12:
                 accepted=True; alpha_prev=t*2? cap
                 break
             t*=0.5
          if not accepted:
             # restart from best with perturbation
             z = best_z + rng.normal(0, noise, n); z-=z.max(); f,score,g=eval; history=[]; alpha_prev=1.0; failed++
             if failed>10: break
             continue
          # update history
          s_vec = (z_new - z)
          y_vec = g_new - g
          sy = np.dot(s_vec, y_vec)
          if sy > 1e-14:
             rho=1/sy; append s,y,rho; maintain max m
          else: maybe clear if fail? For nonconvex, discard pair? Standard still store with damping? We can if sy<=0 clear history to avoid bad.
          z=g_z update; f=...
          if score < best_score: best_score=score; best_z=z.copy(); best_f=f; stall_time=time; print
          elif time-stall_time > 20: # increase beta or perturb
             break? We'll handle outer schedule.
          if time > total_deadline: break
       if best_score <= target: break
    return best_a = softmax(best_z)
```

Two-loop: We need store pairs aligned with current g after each iteration. Implementation:
```
def lbfgs_direction(g, pairs):
    # pairs list of (s,y,rho) oldest to newest
    q = g.copy()
    m=len(pairs)
    alpha = np.zeros(m)
    for k in range(m-1,-1,-1):
        s,y,rho=pairs[k]
        alpha[k] = rho * np.dot(s, q)
        q = q - alpha[k]*y
    # H0
    if m>0:
        s_last, y_last, _ = pairs[m-1]
        yq = np.dot(y_last, q)
        sy = np.dot(s_last, q)
        if sy > 0 and yq > 0:
            r = q * (sy / yq)
        else:
            r = q
        # ensure r not zero
    else:
        r = q
    for k in range(m):
        s,y,rho=pairs[k]
        beta = rho * np.dot(y, r)
        r = r + (alpha[k] - beta) * s
    return r
```
But direction d should be negative? Standard: q starts g, two-loop returns H g, direction = -H g. In code above, q is H g? Let's recall:
Two-loop to compute H g:
q = g
for k=m-1..0: alpha_k = rho_k s_k^T q; q -= alpha_k y_k
if m>0: gamma = (s_k^T q)/(y_k^T q); H0 q = gamma q (or q)
for k=0..m-1: beta_k = rho_k y_k^T r; r += (alpha_k - beta_k) s_k
Direction = -r.
So `r = H g`, direction = -r. If m=0, H0=I, r=q=g, direction=-g. Good. Use direction `d = -r`, ensure slope `g·d <0`. If not, d=-g.
Potential scaling: If yq large, gamma may large. Could clamp gamma? Nonconvex; if not finite, d=-g.

After line accepted, store s = t*d (z update), y = g_new-g. But if d = -r, and H from previous. Good.

Let's design L-BFGS function more concretely:
```
def run_lbfgs(a0, n, budget, rng, beta_schedule, target_score):
    z = np.log(np.clip(a0, 1e-300, None))
    z = z - z.max()
    f, score, g = smooth_fg(z, beta)
    best_z=z.copy(); best_score=score; best_f=f
    pairs=[]
    alpha=1.0
    bad=0
    last_improve=time
    total_deadline=time.budget
    for beta, beta_time in beta_schedule:
       beta_deadline=min(total_deadline, time+beta_time)
       pairs=[]; alpha=1.0; bad=0
       while time.time()<beta_deadline and time.time()<total_deadline:
          if score<=target: break
          # direction
          d = lbfgs_dir(g,pairs)
          slope = np.dot(g,d)
          if not finite or slope >= -1e-300:
             d = -g; slope = -np.dot(g,g)
             if slope ==0: break
          # line search
          step=alpha
          accepted=False
          for _ in range(40):
             z_try = z + step*d
             z_try -= z_try.max()
             f_try, score_try = smooth_f(z_try, beta)
             if f_try <= f + 1e-4*step*slope + 1e-13:
                accepted=True
                # maybe if f_try > f + 0.5*step*slope, accept but not increase
                break
             step *= 0.5
          if not accepted:
             # restart with perturbation from best
             z = best_z + rng.normal(0, 1e-5, n)
             z-=z.max()
             f,score,g=smooth_fg(z,beta)
             pairs=[]; alpha=1.0; bad+=1
             if bad>10: break
             continue
          # If accepted, compute new g
          f_new, score_new, g_new = smooth_fg(z_try,beta)
          # If f_new > f + ... due numerical? accept anyway? Could if f_new not better, restart.
          s_vec = (z_try - z)  # but z_try centered? z original centered; step direction maybe; s = accepted actual difference.
          y_vec = g_new - g
          sy = np.dot(s_vec, y_vec)
          if sy > 1e-14:
              pairs.append((s_vec.copy(), y_vec.copy(), 1.0/sy))
              if len>pairs_max: pairs.pop(0)
              bad=0
          else:
              # discard pair; maybe clear if no curvature
              if len(pairs)>0: pairs.pop()
              bad+=1
              if bad>5: pairs=[]
          z=f?; f=f_new; g=g_new; score=score_new; alpha=max(step*1.5,0.01)?
          if score_new < best_score - 1e-12:
             best_score=score_new; best_z=z_try.copy(); best_f=f_new; last_improve=time
          elif time.time()-last_improve > 30:
             # perturb and maybe reset
             z = best_z + rng.normal(0, 1e-4/np.sqrt(n), n)
             z-=z.max(); f,score,g=smooth_fg(z,beta); pairs=[]; alpha=1.0
          if f_new > f + ...? maybe line search accepted but f_new not? ignore.
       # after beta stage, update best_z maybe carry z=best_z? We can keep current if better.
       z_for_next = best_z? To avoid loss, set z=best_z copy, f,score,g=smooth_fg(z,beta_next) will recomputed in next.
    return softmax(best_z), best_score
```

def initial_candidates(base_a, n, rng):
   cands=[]
   # base normalized/interpolated
   if base_a is not None:
      if len(base_a)==n: a=base_a
      else: a=np.interp(np.linspace(0,1,n), np.linspace(0,1,len(base_a)), base_a)
      a=np.maximum(a,0); s=a.sum(); if s>0: cands.append(a/s)
   # one-sided powers
   i=np.arange(1,n+1)
   for p in [0.5,0.45,0.55,0.35,0.7]:
       a=i**(-p)
       a += 1e-6
       cands.append(a/sum)
   # left-right asymmetric? a_i ∝ (i+1)^-p * exp(-q*(i/n)^2)? maybe.
   # smooth random positive

       a=np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.5,n)); # but too flat? maybe edge spike:
       a = np.exp(-0.5*(np.arange(n)/ (n/20.0))**2) + 1e-6
   # previous best? included.
```

```
def run_accel(start_z, beta, budget):
   z = start_z.copy(); z_prev=z.copy(); t=1; step=1.0 (or estimated)
   best...
   while time:
      t_old=t; t=0.5*(1+sqrt(1+4t*t)); mu=(t_old-1)/t
      y = z + mu*(z-z_prev)
      y -= y.max()
      # compute f_y, score_y, g_y
      # step size adapt: start alpha=1; if t? Use backtracking with f.
      d = -g_y / max(1, norm?) maybe.
      x = y + alpha*d; x-=x.max()
      f_x=smooth_f(x)
      if f_x <= f_y + 1e-4*alpha*(g_y@d): accept; else alpha*=0.5 repeat
      z_prev=z; z=x
```

Pseudo code top-level:
```
import numpy as np
import time

TARGET = 1.5027

def next_pow2(m):
    if m <=1: return 1
    return 1 << (m-1).bit_length()

def softmax_vec(z):
    zm = np.max(z)
    ez = np.exp(z - zm)
    s = ez.sum()
    if s == 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(len(z), 1.0/len(z))
    return ez/s

def square_conv(a, nfft, n):
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, n=nfft)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa.conj(), n=nfft)
    c = c[:2*n-1]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    s = c.sum()
    if s <= 0: return c
    c *= 1.0/s
    return c

def weighted_grad(a, w, nfft, n):
    L = 2*n-1
    wrev = w[::-1]
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, n=nfft)
    fw = np.fft.rfft(wrev, n=nfft)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(fa * fw, n=nfft)
    # h indices
    # Instead of creating idx array each call, use slice/reverse:
    # conv indices L-1, L-2, ..., 0. So h = conv[:L][::-1]? indices from L-1 down to0.
    h = conv[:L][::-1]
    # length L; take first n
    h = h[:n]
    return 2.0*h
```
Check `conv[:L][::-1]` length L; first n gives conv[L-1],conv[L-2],...,conv[L-n], which equals h_i for i=0..L-n? Need h_i = conv[L-1-i]. For i=0..n-1, indices L-1 down to L-n. That's first n of reversed first L. Yes since L=2n-1, L-n=n-1. Wait indices: L=5,n=3 -> i=0 idx4, i=1 idx3, i=2 idx2. `conv[:5][::-1]` = idx4,idx3,idx2,idx1,idx0; first3 correct. Good. For general n, conv indices needed 2n-2 down to n-1. Since L=2n-1, `conv[:L][::-1]` first n gives indices 2n-2 down to n-1. correct. Nice no idx array.
Gradient length n.

`fast_score(a)`:
```
def fast_score(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,float)
   a=np.clip(a,0,None)
   s=a.sum()
   if s < 1e-12: return inf
   if s != 1.0: a=a/s
   n=len(a)
   nb=next_pow2(2*n-1)
   b=square_conv(a,nb,n)
   return 2*n*b.max()
```
This normalizes to sum1. Note evaluator if sum1; if returning sum1, okay. If a has cap? sum1 entries ≤1<1000.

Let's design `lbfgs_dir` carefully.
```
def lbfgs_dir(g, pairs):
    m=len(pairs)
    q=g.copy()
    alphas=np.empty(m)
    for k in range(m-1,-1,-1):
        s,y,rho = pairs[k]
        alphas[k]=rho*np.dot(s,q)
        q = q - alphas[k]*y
    if m>0:
        s,y,rho = pairs[m-1]
        yq = np.dot(y,q)
        if yq > 1e-300:
            sq = np.dot(s,q)
            if sq > 0 and np.isfinite(sq/yq):
                scale = sq/yq
                # clip scale to avoid huge? maybe min(max(scale,1e-6),1e6)
                q = q * scale
            else:
                q = q
        else:
            q = q
    else:
        pass
    r=q
    for k in range(m):
        s,y,rho=pairs[k]
        beta = rho*np.dot(y,r)
        r = r + (alphas[k]-beta)*s
    d = -r
    # If not finite or ascent, fall back
    slope=np.dot(g,d)
    if not np.isfinite(slope) or slope >= 0:
        d = -g
        slope = -np.dot(g,g)
    # If slope zero, return zero
    return d, slope
```
Should H0 scale use last pair with `sy/yq`? If sy from stored pair, but q may changed; standard uses last pair `s_k^T q / y_k^T q`. Good. Clip scale maybe. If scale huge, direction huge, line search shrink. Could be okay. To improve, clip to [1e-8,1e8].

When storing pairs, must convert `s` actual step `z_step` and `y=g_new-g`. Because z centered after step, the step vector has no constant? We center z_try, z original centered; step after centering includes -const component. Since objective invariant to const, aspect? The stored s includes pseudo-constant due centering. But gz sum zero; y sum zero. The constant component in s orthogonal to y? y sum zero; dot unaffected. However s length includes constant changes; L-BFGS may learn null direction but harmless if y zero in null. To be cleaner, after `s_vec = z_try - z`, do `s_vec = s_vec - s_vec.mean()` since constant component doesn't affect a. Similarly ensure y sum zero. But if we project z_try to center, the constant component is exactly `-step*d.mean()`? Wait d from L-BFGS should have zero sum? Since g sum zero and H built from sums? Could still have numerical. Remove mean for stability. If we remove mean from s before storing, for update next direction, constant not needed. Do `s_vec -= s_vec.mean()`, `y_vec -= y_vec.mean()`.

When line search candidate `z_try = z + step*d`, then center. The actual s including centering; after removing mean, equivalent to `step*d_center`. Good.

Function `normalize_nonneg(a)`:
```
def normalize_nonneg(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    a=np.clip(a,0,None)
    s=a.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=1e-15: return np.ones(len(a))/len(a)
    return a/s
```

Let's implement robust `safe_softmat`:
```
def softmax_vec(z):
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)):
       z=np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, -700.0)
    zm=np.max(z)
    if not np.isfinite(zm): z=np.zeros_like(z); zm=0
    ez=np.exp(z-zm)
    s=ez.sum()
    if s<=0 or not finite: ez[:] =1; s=len
    return ez/s
```

def run_lbfgs_start(a0, start_time, duration, ...):

`interpolate_to_length(a, n)`:
```
def interpolate_to_length(a,n):
    a = np.asarray(a,float)
    if len(a)==n: return a.copy()
    m=len(a)
    if m==1: return np.full(n, a[0]/n?)
    x0=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,m)
    x1=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    y=np.interp(x1,x0,a)
    return np.clip(y,0,None)
```
If a sum not1, normalize later.

`fast_score(a, nb=None)`: To use precomputed nb for starts in a length, pass nb to avoid recomputing nextpow2. But fast_score used also in add_candidate with length known. Define:

Selection function:

def select_starts(starts, max_starts):
    starts = [s for s in starts if np.all(np.isfinite(s[0]) and np.isfinite(s[1]))]
    if not starts: return []
    starts.sort(key=start_score_key)
    selected=[]
    # best overall
    selected.append(starts[0])
    # best non-reference
    for item in starts:
       name=item[2]
       if not name.startswith(('best','base','noise')):
          selected.append(item); break
    # best noise
    for item in starts:
       if item[2].startswith('noise') and item not in selected: selected.append(item); break
    # base
    for item in starts:
       if item[2]=='base' and item not in selected: selected.append(item); break
    for item in starts:
       if item not in selected: selected.append(item)
       if len(selected)==max_starts: break
    # remove duplicates references? list identity works.
    return selected[:max_starts]
```

Now, code details:
```
def next_pow2(m):
    if m <= 1: return 1
    return 1 << (int(m) - 1).bit_length()
```
Use int m.

`softmax_vec` with floor:
```
def softmax_vec(z):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)):
       z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, -700.0)
    zm = float(np.max(z))
    if not np.isfinite(zm):
       return np.full(len(z), 1.0/len(z))
    ez = np.exp(z - zm)
    ez = np.maximum(ez, 1e-300)
    s = float(np.sum(ez))
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
       return np.full(len(z), 1.0/len(z))
    ez /= s
    return ez
```
If n large, floor sum ~n*1e-300 negligible. Good.

`square_conv`:
```
def square_conv(a, nfft, n):
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, n=nfft)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa.conj(), n=nfft)
    L = 2*n - 1
    c = c[:L]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(c))
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return c
    c *= (1.0 / s)
    return c
```
If nfft < L? next_pow2 ensures.

`weighted_grad`:
```
def weighted_grad(a, w, nfft, n):
    L=2*n-1
    wrev = w[::-1]
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,n=nfft)
    fw=np.fft.rfft(wrev,n=nfft)
    conv=np.fft.irfft(fa*fw,n=nfft)
    h = conv[:L][::-1][:n]
    return 2.0*h
```
Need ensure conv has at least L elements. Ng≥3n-2 >L. Good. `conv[:L]` length L, reversed. `[:n]` length n.

`smooth_f`:
```
def smooth_f(z, beta, n, nb, ng):
    a=softmax_vec(z)
    b=square_conv(a,nb,n)
    mb=float(np.max(b))
    x=beta*(b-mb)
    # clip x max 0
    # If beta large and x very negative, exp okay
    ex=np.exp(x)
    s=float(np.sum(ex))
    if s <=0 or not finite: s=1.0
    f = mb + np.log(s)/beta
    score = 2.0*n*mb
    W=2.0*n
    return W*f, W*score
```
`ng` unused except for consistency; could remove argument but keep. Actually smooth_f doesn't need ng. Define with ng param ignored? Maybe omit. But run_lbfgs calls with same args? We can define `smooth_f(z,beta,n,nb)` no ng. Top-level. Good.

`smooth_fg`:
```
def smooth_fg(z,beta,n,nb,ng):
   a=softmax_vec(z)
   b=square_conv(a,nb,n)
   mb=float(np.max(b))
   x=beta*(b-mb)
   ex=np.exp(x); s=float(np.sum(ex)); score=2n*mb
   if s<=0 or not finite:
       p=np.zeros(b.shape); p[int(np.argmax(b))]=1.0
   else: p=ex/s
   f = mb + np.log(s)/beta
   ga=weighted_grad(a,p,ng,n)
   dot=float(np.dot(a,ga))
   W=2.0*n
   gz = W * (a * (ga - dot))
   gz -= float(np.mean(gz))
   gz = np.where(np.isfinite(gz), gz, 0.0)
   return W*f, score, gz
```
Score returned not multiplied? We return true score (not W*score? true score is score itself: 2n*mb). Wait `score = 2.0*n*mb` already true. f_score = W*f (since W=2n). Return `(f_score, score, gz)`. Good.

Potential: If beta very large, `np.log(s)` where s could be 1 plus underflows; okay.

`smooth_f` return true score already: `score=2n*mb`, not W*score. `f_score=W*f`. Good.

In run, keep variables `f` (smooth score), `score` (true max score), `g`.

Now, line search `f_trial, score_trial = smooth_f(z_try,beta,n,nb)`. It returns score but not used except maybe. If f_trial NaN, fail.

If beta changes, f values not comparable across stages; reset.

`run_lbfgs_start` detailed code:
```
def run_lbfgs_start(a0, deadline, rng, n, nb, ng, init_score, name):
    now0=time.monotonic()
    duration=deadline-now0
    a0=normalize_nonneg(a0)
    if not np.isfinite(init_score): init_score=fast_score(a0,nb)
    print(...)
    if duration <=1: return a0, init_score
    z=np.log(a0 + 1e-300)
    z -= float(np.max(z))
    M0=max(init_score/(2.0*n), 1e-12)
    beta0=max(2000.0, min(800000.0, 8.0/M0))
    betas=[min(1000000.0, beta0), min(1000000.0, 5.0*beta0), min(1000000.0, 25.0*beta0)]
    # unique
    ub=[]
    for b in betas:
       if not ub or b > ub[-1]*1.05: ub.append(b)
    if len(ub)==1: fracs=[0.0,1.0]
    elif len(ub)==2: fracs=[0.0,0.3,1.0]
    else: fracs=[0.0,0.2,0.55,1.0]
    best_z=z.copy(); best_score=init_score; best_f=None
    # compute initial f,g for best_z under first beta? We can set z=best_z, f,score,g = smooth_fg for ub[0]
    total_end=deadline
    pairs_max=18
    for stage_idx, beta in enumerate(ub):
       stage_start = now0 + duration*fracs[stage_idx]
       stage_end = now0 + duration*fracs[stage_idx+1]
       if stage_idx==len(ub)-1: stage_end=total_end
       stage_end=min(stage_end,total_end)
       if time.monotonic() >= stage_end: break
       # start from best_z
       z=best_z.copy()
       f,score,g=smooth_fg(z,beta,n,nb,ng)
       if score < best_score: best_score=score; best_z=z.copy(); best_f=f
       pairs=[]; alpha=1.0; fail_count=0; bad_pairs=0
       last_improve=time.monotonic()
       perturb_count=0
       stall_limit=max(8.0,min(40.0,duration*0.03+10))
       while time.monotonic() < stage_end and time.monotonic() < total_end:
          if score <= best_score: # current best
             pass
          gnorm=np.sqrt(float(np.dot(g,g)))
          if not np.isfinite(gnorm) or gnorm < 1e-10:
             # likely converged in this beta
             break
          d,slope=lbfgs_direction(g,pairs)
          if not (np.all(np.isfinite(d))) or slope > -1e-300:
             d = -g; slope = -gnorm*gnorm
          if slope > -1e-15 or not finite:
             # perturb or break
             fail_count += 3
          else:
             # center d
             d = d - float(np.mean(d))
             # line search
             step=alpha
             accepted=False
             z_try=None
             f_trial=score_trial=None
             for _ in range(45):
                 z_try = z + step*d
                 if not np.all(np.isfinite(z_try)):
                    step *= 0.5; continue
                 z_try -= float(np.max(z_try))
                 f_trial, score_trial = smooth_f(z_try,beta,n,nb)
                 if np.isfinite(f_trial) and f_trial <= f + 1e-4*step*slope + 1e-9:
                     accepted=True; break
                 step *= 0.5
             if not accepted:
                 fail_count += 1
                 if fail_count == 3:
                    pairs=[]; alpha=max(0.1, alpha*0.5)
                 elif fail_count >= 6:
                    # perturb
                    sigma = 0.05 if perturb_count<3 else 0.02
                    z = best_z + rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n)
                    z -= float(np.max(z))
                    f,score,g = smooth_fg(z,beta,n,nb,ng)
                    pairs=[]; alpha=1.0; fail_count=0; perturb_count +=1
                    if score < best_score: best...
                    if perturb_count >= 12: break
                continue
             # accept
             f_new, score_new, g_new = smooth_fg(z_try,beta,n,nb,ng)
             if not np.isfinite(f_new) or not np.isfinite(score_new):
                 # should not
                 fail_count +=1; continue
             s_vec = z_try - z
             s_vec -= float(np.mean(s_vec))
             y_vec = g_new - g
             y_vec -= float(np.mean(y_vec))
             sy = float(np.dot(s_vec,y_vec))
             if sy > 1e-14:
                 pairs.append((s_vec, y_vec, 1.0/sy))
                 if len(pairs)>pairs_max: pairs.pop(0)
                 bad_pairs=0
             else:
                 bad_pairs +=1
                 if bad_pairs>4:
                    pairs=[]; bad_pairs=0
             # update
             z = z_try; f=f_new; g=g_new# score is variable from unpack? Need assign score=score_new
             score = score_new
             # alpha update
             if accepted:
                if step >= alpha*0.9:
                    alpha = min(100.0, alpha*1.5)
                else:
                    alpha = max(0.1, step*1.2)
             # best
             if score < best_score:
                 best_score=score; best_z=z.copy(); best_f=f; last_improve=time.monotonic()
                 print maybe every improvement? Could be too much; print if score < ... threshold
             elif time.monotonic() - last_improve > stall_limit:
                 sigma=0.03
                 z = best_z + rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n)
                 z-=float(np.max(z))
                 f,score,g=smooth_fg(z,beta,n,nb,ng)
                 pairs=[]; fail_count=0; bad_pairs=0
                 # update last_improve
                 last_improve=time.monotonic()
                 perturb_count +=1
                 if perturb_count >= 15: break
       # end stage
       now=time.monotonic()
       if now < total_end and best_score < 1.52: continue
    # end stages
    best_a=softmax_vec(best_z)
    best_sc=fast_score(best_a,nb) # verify exact
    if best_sc < best_score: best_score=best_sc
    return best_a, best_score
```

```python
"""
Search for low-maximum autocorrelation nonnegative sequences.

Objective:
    2*n*max(conv(a,a)) / sum(a)^2

Works on the probability simplex (sum(a)=1).  The main optimizer is a
custom BFGS / L-BFGS in log-softmax coordinates, minimizing a smooth
maximum approximation of the convolution peak.  Convolutions and
convolution gradients are computed with FFT.  Multiple initializations,
restarts, beta continuation, and a few different sequence lengths are
tried within the time budget.
"""

import numpy as np
import time

TARGET = 1.5027
MAX_N = 15000


def next_pow2(m):
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def normalize_nonneg(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-15:
        return np.full(a.shape, 1.0 / max(1, a.size), dtype=np.float64)
    a *= 1.0 / s
    return a


def make_power(n, p):
    i = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.power(i, -p)
    return normalize_nonneg(a)


def interpolate_to_length(a, n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == n:
        return a.copy()
    if a.size == 1:
        return np.full(n, float(a[0]) / max(1, n), dtype=np.float64)
    x0 = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, a.size)
    x1 = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    y = np.interp(x1, x0, a)
    return np.clip(y, 0.0, None)


def softmax_vec(z):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)):
        z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, -700.0).astype(np.float64)
    zm = float(np.max(z))
    if not np.isfinite(zm):
        return np.full(z.shape, 1.0 / z.size, dtype=np.float64)
    ez = np.exp(z - zm)
    ez = np.maximum(ez, 1e-300)
    s = float(np.sum(ez))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(z.shape, 1.0 / z.size, dtype=np.float64)
    ez /= s
    return ez


def square_conv(a, nfft, n):
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, n=nfft)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa.conj(), n=nfft)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    c = c[:L]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(c))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return c
    c *= 1.0 / s
    return c


def fast_score(a, nb=None):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.01:
        return np.inf
    a = a / s
    n = len(a)
    if nb is None:
        nb = next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    b = square_conv(a, nb, n)
    return float(2.0 * n * np.max(b))


def weighted_grad(a, w, nfft, n):
    """
    Gradient wrt a of sum_k w_k * (conv(a,a))_k.
    For each lag k, d b_k / d a_i = 2 * sum_j valid a_j for k=i+j.
    """
    L = 2 * n - 1
    wrev = w[::-1]
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, n=nfft)
    fw = np.fft.rfft(wrev, n=nfft)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(fa * fw, n=nfft)
    h = conv[:L][::-1][:n]
    return 2.0 * h


def smooth_f(z, beta, n, nb):
    a = softmax_vec(z)
    b = square_conv(a, nb, n)
    mb = float(np.max(b))
    x = beta * (b - mb)
    ex = np.exp(x)
    s = float(np.sum(ex))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        s = 1.0
    f = mb + np.log(s) / beta
    score = 2.0 * n * mb
    W = 2.0 * n
    return W * f, score


def smooth_fg(z, beta, n, nb, ng):
    a = softmax_vec(z)
    b = square_conv(a, nb, n)
    mb = float(np.max(b))
    x = beta * (b - mb)
    ex = np.exp(x)
    s = float(np.sum(ex))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        p = np.zeros_like(b)
        p[int(np.argmax(b))] = 1.0
        s = 1.0
    else:
        p = ex / s
    f = mb + np.log(s) / beta
    score = 2.0 * n * mb

    ga = weighted_grad(a, p, ng, n)
    dot_ag = float(np.dot(a, ga))
    W = 2.0 * n

    # Gradient of F(z) = W * smooth_max(softmax(z)) wrt z:
    # dz_i = a_i * (g_i - sum_j a_j g_j)
    gz = W * (a * (ga - dot_ag))
    gz -= float(np.mean(gz))
    gz = np.where(np.isfinite(gz), gz, 0.0)
    return W * f, score, gz


def lbfgs_direction(g, pairs):
    m = len(pairs)
    if m == 0:
        d = -g
        slope = -float(np.dot(g, g))
        return d, slope

    q = g.copy()
    alphas = np.empty(m, dtype=np.float64)

    for k in range(m - 1, -1, -1):
        s, y, rho = pairs[k]
        alphas[k] = rho * float(np.dot(s, q))
        q = q - alphas[k] * y

    if m > 0:
        s_last, y_last, _ = pairs[m - 1]
        yq = float(np.dot(y_last, q))
        if yq > 1e-300:
            sq = float(np.dot(s_last, q))
            if sq > 0.0 and np.isfinite(sq / yq):
                scale = sq / yq
                if scale > 1e6:
                    scale = 1e6
                elif scale < 1e-6:
                    scale = 1e-6
                q = q * scale

    for k in range(m):
        s, y, rho = pairs[k]
        beta = rho * float(np.dot(y, q))
        q = q + (alphas[k] - beta) * s

    d = -q
    slope = float(np.dot(g, d))
    if not np.isfinite(slope) or slope >= 0.0:
        d = -g
        slope = -float(np.dot(g, g))
    return d, slope


def run_lbfgs_start(a0, deadline, rng, n, nb, ng, init_score, name):
    now0 = time.monotonic()
    duration = max(0.0, deadline - now0)
    if duration <= 2.0:
        return a0, init_score

    a0 = normalize_nonneg(a0)
    if not np.isfinite(init_score):
        init_score = fast_score(a0, nb)

    print(f"  L-BFGS start '{name}' n={n} init={init_score:.8f} dur={duration:.0f}s")

    z = np.log(a0 + 1e-300)
    z -= float(np.max(z))

    M0 = max(init_score / (2.0 * n), 1e-12)
    beta0 = max(2000.0, min(800000.0, 8.0 / M0))
    raw_betas = [
        min(1000000.0, beta0),
        min(1000000.0, 5.0 * beta0),
        min(1000000.0, 25.0 * beta0),
    ]

    betas = []
    for b in raw_betas:
        if not betas or b > betas[-1] * 1.05:
            betas.append(b)

    if len(betas) == 1:
        fracs = [0.0, 1.0]
    elif len(betas) == 2:
        fracs = [0.0, 0.30, 1.0]
    else:
        fracs = [0.0, 0.20, 0.55, 1.0]

    best_z = z.copy()
    best_score = init_score
    total_end = min(deadline, now0 + duration)
    pairs_max = 18

    for stage_idx, beta in enumerate(betas):
        stage_start = now0 + duration * fracs[stage_idx]
        stage_end = now0 + duration * fracs[stage_idx + 1]
        if stage_idx == len(betas) - 1:
            stage_end = total_end
        stage_end = min(stage_end, total_end)
        if time.monotonic() >= stage_end:
            break

        z = best_z.copy()
        f, score, g = smooth_fg(z, beta, n, nb, ng)
        if score < best_score:
            best_score = score
            best_z = z.copy()

        pairs = []
        alpha = 1.0
        fail_count = 0
        bad_pairs = 0
        perturb_count = 0
        last_improve = time.monotonic()
        stall_limit = max(8.0, min(40.0, 10.0 + 0.03 * duration))

        while time.monotonic() < stage_end and time.monotonic() < total_end:
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
                z = best_z + rng.normal(0.0, 0.05, n)
                z -= float(np.max(z))
                f, score, g = smooth_fg(z, beta, n, nb, ng)
                pairs = []
                fail_count = 0
                perturb_count += 1
                if perturb_count > 15:
                    break
                continue

            gnorm2 = float(np.dot(g, g))
            if not np.isfinite(gnorm2) or gnorm2 < 1e-20:
                break

            d, slope = lbfgs_direction(g, pairs)
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(d)) or slope > -1e-300:
                d = -g
                slope = -gnorm2

            if not np.isfinite(slope) or slope > -1e-15:
                fail_count += 2
            else:
                d = d - float(np.mean(d))
                step = alpha
                accepted = False
                z_try = None
                f_trial = np.inf
                score_trial = np.inf

                for _ in range(45):
                    z_try = z + step * d
                    if not np.all(np.isfinite(z_try)):
                        step *= 0.5
                        continue
                    z_try -= float(np.max(z_try))
                    f_trial, score_trial = smooth_f(z_try, beta, n, nb)
                    if np.isfinite(f_trial) and f_trial <= f + 1e-4 * step * slope + 1e-10:
                        accepted = True
                        break
                    step *= 0.5

                if not accepted or z_try is None:
                    fail_count += 1
                    if fail_count == 3:
                        pairs = []
                        alpha = max(0.1, alpha * 0.5)
                    elif fail_count >= 6:
                        sigma = 0.05 if perturb_count < 3 else 0.02
                        z = best_z + rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n)
                        z -= float(np.max(z))
                        f, score, g = smooth_fg(z, beta, n, nb, ng)
                        pairs = []
                        alpha = 1.0
                        fail_count = 0
                        perturb_count += 1
                        if score < best_score:
                            best_score = score
                            best_z = z.copy()
                        if perturb_count >= 15:
                            break
                    continue

                f_new, score_new, g_new = smooth_fg(z_try, beta, n, nb, ng)
                if not np.isfinite(f_new) or not np.isfinite(score_new):
                    fail_count += 1
                    continue

                s_vec = z_try - z
                s_vec -= float(np.mean(s_vec))
                y_vec = g_new - g
                y_vec -= float(np.mean(y_vec))
                sy = float(np.dot(s_vec, y_vec))

                if sy > 1e-14:
                    pairs.append((s_vec, y_vec, 1.0 / sy))
                    if len(pairs) > pairs_max:
                        pairs.pop(0)
                    bad_pairs = 0
                else:
                    bad_pairs += 1
                    if bad_pairs > 4:
                        pairs = []
                        bad_pairs = 0

                z = z_try
                f = f_new
                g = g_new
                score = score_new

                if step >= alpha * 0.9:
                    alpha = min(100.0, alpha * 1.5)
                else:
                    alpha = max(0.1, step * 1.2)

                if score < best_score:
                    best_score = score
                    best_z = z.copy()
                    last_improve = time.monotonic()

            if time.monotonic() - last_improve > stall_limit:
                sigma = 0.03
                z = best_z + rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n)
                z -= float(np.max(z))
                f, score, g = smooth_fg(z, beta, n, nb, ng)
                pairs = []
                fail_count = 0
                bad_pairs = 0
                perturb_count += 1
                last_improve = time.monotonic()
                if score < best_score:
                    best_score = score
                    best_z = z.copy()
                if perturb_count >= 20:
                    break

        print(f"    stage beta={beta:.0f} best={best_score:.8f} elapsed={time.monotonic()-now0:.0f}s")

    best_a = softmax_vec(best_z)
    best_sc = fast_score(best_a, nb)
    if np.isfinite(best_sc) and best_sc < best_score:
        best_score = best_sc
    return best_a, best_score


def start_score_key(item):
    return item[1]


def add_start(starts, a, name, nb):
    a = normalize_nonneg(a)
    sc = fast_score(a, nb)
    if np.isfinite(sc):
        starts.append((a, sc, name))


def make_starts(best_a, base_a, n, rng, nb, max_starts):
    starts = []

    if best_a is not None:
        add_start(starts, interpolate_to_length(best_a, n), "best", nb)
    if base_a is not None:
        add_start(starts, interpolate_to_length(base_a, n), "base", nb)

    ref = None
    if best_a is not None:
        ref = interpolate_to_length(best_a, n)
    elif base_a is not None:
        ref = interpolate_to_length(base_a, n)

    if ref is not None:
        for idx, sigma in enumerate((0.02, 0.05, 0.10, 0.20)):
            noise = rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n)
            a = ref * np.exp(noise)
            add_start(starts, a, f"noise{idx}", nb)

    i = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    for p in (0.45, 0.50, 0.55, 0.65, 0.35):
        add_start(starts, i ** (-p), f"power{p}", nb)

    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    for rate in (1.0, 4.0, 12.0):
        add_start(starts, np.exp(-rate * x), f"exp{rate}", nb)

    add_start(starts, np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.35, n)), "rand", nb)

    # Sort and select a small diverse set.
    starts.sort(key=start_score_key)
    selected = []
    used_names = set()

    if starts:
        selected.append(starts[0])
        used_names.add(starts[0][2])

    for item in starts:
        nm = item[2]
        if nm not in used_names and (nm.startswith("power") or nm.startswith("exp") or nm.startswith("rand")):
            selected.append(item)
            used_names.add(nm)
            break

    for item in starts:
        nm = item[2]
        if nm not in used_names and nm.startswith("noise"):
            selected.append(item)
            used_names.add(nm)
            break

    for item in starts:
        nm = item[2]
        if nm not in used_names and nm == "base":
            selected.append(item)
            used_names.add(nm)
            break

    for item in starts:
        nm = item[2]
        if nm not in used_names:
            selected.append(item)
            used_names.add(nm)
        if len(selected) >= max_starts:
            break

    return selected[:max_starts]


def alloc_fractions(k):
    if k <= 0:
        return []
    if k == 1:
        return [1.0]
    if k == 2:
        return [0.60, 0.40]
    if k == 3:
        return [0.50, 0.30, 0.20]
    if k == 4:
        return [0.45, 0.30, 0.15, 0.10]
    return [0.42, 0.26, 0.14, 0.10, 0.08][:k]


def search_length(length, deadline, rng, base_global, best_a, best_score):
    now = time.monotonic()
    if length <= 1 or now >= deadline:
        return best_a, best_score

    nb = next_pow2(2 * length - 1)
    ng = next_pow2(3 * length - 2)
    duration = deadline - now

    if duration > 800:
        max_starts = 5
    elif duration > 400:
        max_starts = 4
    elif duration > 150:
        max_starts = 3
    else:
        max_starts = 2

    starts = make_starts(best_a, base_global, length, rng, nb, max_starts)
    if not starts:
        return best_a, best_score

    fracs = alloc_fractions(len(starts))

    for idx, (a, sc, nm) in enumerate(starts):
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now >= deadline:
            break
        remaining = deadline - now
        frac = fracs[idx]
        this_deadline = min(deadline, now + remaining * frac)
        if idx == len(fracs) - 1:
            this_deadline = deadline
        if this_deadline - now <= 5.0:
            continue

        new_a, new_score = run_lbfgs_start(
            a, this_deadline, rng, length, nb, ng, sc, nm
        )
        if np.isfinite(new_score) and new_score < best_score:
            best_score = new_score
            best_a = new_a
            print(f"  new global best (n={length}): {best_score:.8f}")

    now = time.monotonic()
    if best_a is not None and now + 80.0 < deadline and best_score > (TARGET - 0.01):
        polish_deadline = min(deadline, now + max(60.0, 0.5 * (deadline - now)))
        new_a, new_score = run_lbfgs_start(
            best_a, polish_deadline, rng, length, nb, ng, best_score, "polish"
        )
        if np.isfinite(new_score) and new_score < best_score:
            best_score = new_score
            best_a = new_a
            print(f"  polish improved (n={length}): {best_score:.8f}")

    return best_a, best_score


def zero_refine(a, nb):
    a = normalize_nonneg(a)
    best = a
    best_sc = fast_score(a, nb)
    for th in (1e-12, 1e-10, 1e-8, 1e-5, 1e-4):
        a2 = a.copy()
        a2[a2 < th] = 0.0
        a2 = normalize_nonneg(a2)
        sc2 = fast_score(a2, nb)
        if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_sc:
            best = a2
            best_sc = sc2
    return best, best_sc


def propose_candidate():
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    hard_end = t0 + min(2400.0, 2370.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(123456789)

    base = None
    try:
        base = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        base = None

    if base is not None:
        base = np.clip(base, 0.0, None)
        if base.size == 0:
            base = None

    if base is None:
        n_base = 4806
        base = make_power(n_base, 0.5)
    else:
        n_base = int(base.size)
        if n_base > MAX_N:
            base = normalize_nonneg(interpolate_to_length(base, MAX_N))
            n_base = MAX_N
        if n_base < 2:
            n_base = 4806
            base = make_power(n_base, 0.5)
        base = normalize_nonneg(base)

    n_list = [n_base]
    alt = min(MAX_N, max(2 * n_base, n_base + 2000))
    if alt > n_base:
        n_list.append(alt)

    best_a = None
    best_score = np.inf

    if len(n_list) == 1:
        deadline1 = hard_end
    else:
        deadline1 = t0 + 0.62 * (hard_end - t0)

    best_a, best_score = search_length(n_list[0], deadline1, rng, base, best_a, best_score)
    print(f"  after n={n_list[0]}: best={best_score:.8f}")

    if len(n_list) > 1:
        if time.monotonic() < hard_end - 60:
            best_a, best_score = search_length(n_list[1], hard_end, rng, base, best_a, best_score)
            print(f"  after n={n_list[1]}: best={best_score:.8f}")

    if best_a is None:
        best_a = base

    best_a = normalize_nonneg(best_a)
    nb = next_pow2(2 * len(best_a) - 1)
    best_a, best_score = zero_refine(best_a, nb)

    final_list = [float(x) for x in best_a if np.isfinite(x)]
    final_list = [max(0.0, v) for v in final_list]
    s = float(np.sum(final_list))
    if s < 0.01:
        final_list = [float(x) for x in make_power(4806, 0.5)]
    else:
        final_list = [v / s for v in final_list]

    final_sc = fast_score(np.asarray(final_list, dtype=np.float64), nb)
    print(f"  internal final score: {final_sc:.8f}")

    if len(final_list) <= 12000 and time.monotonic() < hard_end - 15:
        try:
            ev = evaluate_sequence(final_list)
            if np.isfinite(ev):
                print(f"  evaluator final score: {ev:.8f}")
        except Exception:
            pass

    print(f"  returning n={len(final_list)} score~{final_sc:.8f}")
    return final_list
```
